# The Second Beast - The False Prophet (Revelation 13)

**[00:00:00]** The Apostle John saw a vision of a beast that looked like a lamb but spoke like a dragon.

**[00:00:04]** This beast, which is a political power, would fool people in the end times into building a

**[00:00:09]** religio-political system that would honor the first beast from the sea. Today we're going to

**[00:00:15]** look at who this beast is and how it has fulfilled this role in history and how today it is helping

**[00:00:21]** to build the one world system that is soon to come.

**[00:00:30]** Hey everybody, welcome back to the Dance of Life podcast. My name is Tudor Alexander and I'm your

**[00:00:37]** host today. Thanks so much for being with me on this wonderfully hot summer day. At least it is

**[00:00:42]** for me. It's about 108 degrees outside, which is just crazy. I live in Phoenix, Arizona. So hope

**[00:00:48]** your weather is better than mine, but in either case, thanks for joining me. Today we're talking

**[00:00:53]** about the second beast that John saw in the book of Revelation. This is chapter 13 of the book of

**[00:00:59]** Revelation.

**[00:01:00]** He saw two beasts. The first one was from the sea and we talked about that extensively,

**[00:01:03]** how that first beast that ruled for 1260 years was the papacy, was the religio-political system

**[00:01:10]** of the papacy. And in today's episode, we're going to be taking a deeper dive into a

**[00:01:16]** less understood and less discussed part of that chapter, chapter 13, which is the second beast.

**[00:01:24]** The second beast comes from the earth and we'll get into it today. But this second beast looks

**[00:01:29]** like a lamb.

**[00:01:30]** Which is Christianity. Only Christianity or Jesus was basically resembled by the lamb. So if this

**[00:01:36]** system, this political power looks like a lamb, it means it looks Christian, but yet it speaks

**[00:01:42]** like a dragon. And we know that political powers are beasts and when beasts speak,

**[00:01:47]** they do so through their laws. So whatever this power is in history, it looks Christian,

**[00:01:52]** but yet it speaks or legislates in the opposite direction. And so that's really important to

**[00:01:58]** understand because

**[00:01:59]** ultimately that means we will get deceived. And if we read through Revelation 13,

**[00:02:05]** this second beast acts like a false prophet for the first beast, meaning that it fools the world

**[00:02:11]** into building an image to this first beast. Now, all these things sound a little hard to understand

**[00:02:20]** if we're thinking literally like a literal person as a false prophet. But again,

**[00:02:26]** all the episodes up until now. So if you're just joining me,

**[00:02:29]** please go review them, check them out. There's so many wonderful resources for you. But really,

**[00:02:33]** the point is this, a beast is a kingdom. It's a political power. And so it's not an individual

**[00:02:38]** acting as a false prophet to some other individual. It is a system that acts as a false prophet to

**[00:02:45]** another system. And this is the thing to understand from today. But nevertheless, this system,

**[00:02:51]** the second beast, fools people in the end times into building an image to the first beast. Now,

**[00:02:56]** an image is a representation.

**[00:02:59]** It is a, now in normal speaking, it's a statue, right? It's an image of something

**[00:03:06]** that represents a god or a goddess of some kind, and people worship it. So it's a representation.

**[00:03:12]** We were made in God's image. We're to bear his image. We are representatives of God.

**[00:03:17]** So images are used throughout the Bible. But when we look at this book of Revelation,

**[00:03:22]** which is very cryptic in its writing, it's very apocalyptic writing, very symbolic, right? So

**[01:14:09]** to take a punishment that he

**[01:14:11]** did not deserve. We deserved it.

**[01:14:13]** And to save

**[01:14:15]** our souls. He didn't care about that.

**[01:14:17]** He didn't care about being born again and having

**[01:14:19]** your heart changed. He cared about, okay, well,

**[01:14:21]** I guess if it helps society do better

**[01:14:23]** works, then, you know, sure, you can believe

**[01:14:25]** that. So, Ben Franklin,

**[01:14:27]** definitely not a Christian.

**[01:14:29]** Let's move on to

**[01:14:31]** George Washington.

**[01:14:33]** Now, I want to draw to your

**[01:14:35]** attention a bust

**[01:14:37]** of George Washington. You can look this up on Google.

**[01:14:39]** It's in Holton,

**[01:14:41]** Maine. Spelled

**[01:14:43]** H-O-U-L-T-O-N.

**[01:14:45]** Now, this is a bust of George Washington

**[01:14:47]** that's pretty famous. And

**[01:14:49]** it's famous for a lot of reasons, but it's

**[01:14:51]** this bust that's on top of a block

**[01:14:53]** of a black cube that commemorates

**[01:14:55]** George Washington as a Freemason.

**[01:14:57]** It's kind of hard to see in this picture,

**[01:14:59]** but you have the Freemason compass

**[01:15:01]** and logo, whatever, there.

**[01:15:03]** The square and compass.

**[01:15:05]** And then you have

**[01:15:07]** Freemason on the top of this

**[01:15:09]** and first president.

**[01:15:11]** So, there's a couple things here. If you know anything about

**[01:15:13]** the black cube, I'm not going to go

**[01:15:15]** into that today. We're going to go into that in a future episode

**[01:15:17]** when we talk about Islam, but

**[01:15:19]** the black cube is definitely an occult

**[01:15:21]** symbol that all of them use everywhere.

**[01:15:23]** It's just, it's not put there

**[01:15:25]** by accident. They had a lot of ways that they could have

**[01:15:27]** supported this bust,

**[01:15:29]** but yet it was put on a black cube.

**[01:15:31]** And then you have the Freemason symbol

**[01:15:33]** and you have Freemason on top of

**[01:15:35]** first president. So who came first?

**[01:15:37]** Freemason or first president?

**[01:15:39]** It's Freemason. You can look up

**[01:15:41]** this cube for yourself, but

**[01:15:43]** that's a pretty telling sign of where

**[01:15:45]** George Washington stands.

**[01:15:47]** But you also have this apotheosis

**[01:15:49]** of Washington, which is a

**[01:15:51]** fresco painted by

**[01:15:53]** an artist named Constantino Brumidi

**[01:15:55]** in the 1800s, and it's

**[01:15:57]** painted in the Capitol building.

**[01:15:59]** Apotheosis means to become like

**[01:16:01]** God, to be like God.

**[01:16:03]** And apotheosis of Washington means

**[01:16:05]** that, oh, well, Washington basically became God.

**[01:16:07]** And if you look at the images of

**[01:16:09]** this apotheosis,

**[01:16:11]** I mean, basically you have

**[01:16:13]** George Washington in the clouds, and he's

**[01:16:15]** surrounded by angels, and

**[01:16:17]** he's like this godlike figure

**[01:16:19]** holding his hand

**[01:16:21]** out, and he's got a sword in his hand.

**[01:16:23]** There's a lot of occult symbolism. Again, you have

**[01:16:27]** the Roman fasci, which we talked about with the

**[01:16:29]** declarations of the human rights

**[01:16:31]** and citizen from the French Revolution

**[01:16:39]** all these, you know,

**[01:16:43]** just prevalent everywhere. So, anyway,

**[01:16:45]** the point is this.
